\Hypertarget{page__alloc_8c_a56039f9e09a38ab23be44bebebcbb8eb}\index{page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}!alloc\_contig\_frozen\_pages\_noprof@{alloc\_contig\_frozen\_pages\_noprof}}
\index{alloc\_contig\_frozen\_pages\_noprof@{alloc\_contig\_frozen\_pages\_noprof}!page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{alloc\_contig\_frozen\_pages\_noprof()}{alloc\_contig\_frozen\_pages\_noprof()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{page__alloc_8c_a56039f9e09a38ab23be44bebebcbb8eb} 
struct page \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+pages\+\_\+noprof (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{unsigned long}]{nr\+\_\+pages}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp\+\_\+mask}{, }\item[{int}]{nid}{, }\item[{nodemask\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{nodemask}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+pages() -- tries to find and allocate contiguous range of frozen pages @nr\+\_\+pages:\+ Number of contiguous pages to allocate @gfp\+\_\+mask:\+ GFP mask. Node/\+zone/\+placement hints limit the search; only some action and reclaim modifiers are supported. Reclaim modifiers control allocation behavior during compaction/\+migration/\+reclaim. @nid:\+ Target node @nodemask:\+ Mask for other possible nodes

This routine is a wrapper around alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+range(). It scans over zones on an applicable zonelist to find a contiguous pfn range which can then be tried for allocation with alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+range(). This routine is intended for allocation requests which can not be fulfilled with the buddy allocator.

The allocated memory is always aligned to a page boundary. If nr\+\_\+pages is a power of two, then allocated range is also guaranteed to be aligned to same nr\+\_\+pages (e.\+g. 1GB request would be aligned to 1GB).

Allocated frozen pages need be freed with \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_aa834ac7e91351ceacefdedd543fe0147}{free\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+range()}, or by manually calling \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_afcaf6e63811188badf0f7b9802c8963a}{free\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+pages()} on each allocated frozen non-\/compound page, for compound frozen pages could be freed with \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_afcaf6e63811188badf0f7b9802c8963a}{free\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+pages()} directly.

Return:\+ pointer to contiguous frozen pages on success, or NULL if not successful.

\Hypertarget{page__alloc_8c_ac112ada2f4a0184f9451da7c4526b03b}\index{page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}!alloc\_contig\_frozen\_range\_noprof@{alloc\_contig\_frozen\_range\_noprof}}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{alloc\_contig\_frozen\_range\_noprof()}{alloc\_contig\_frozen\_range\_noprof()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{page__alloc_8c_ac112ada2f4a0184f9451da7c4526b03b} 
int alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+range\+\_\+noprof (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{unsigned long}]{start}{, }\item[{unsigned long}]{end}{, }\item[{acr\+\_\+flags\+\_\+t}]{alloc\+\_\+flags}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp\+\_\+mask}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+range() -- tries to allocate given range of frozen pages @start:\+ start PFN to allocate @end:\+ one-\/past-\/the-\/last PFN to allocate @alloc\+\_\+flags:\+ allocation information @gfp\+\_\+mask:\+ GFP mask. Node/\+zone/\+placement hints are ignored; only some action and reclaim modifiers are supported. Reclaim modifiers control allocation behavior during compaction/\+migration/\+reclaim.

The PFN range does not have to be pageblock aligned. The PFN range must belong to a single zone.

The first thing this routine does is attempt to MIGRATE\+\_\+\+ISOLATE all pageblocks in the range. Once isolated, the pageblocks should not be modified by others.

All frozen pages which PFN is in \+[start, end) are allocated for the caller, and they could be freed with \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_aa834ac7e91351ceacefdedd543fe0147}{free\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+range()}, \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_afcaf6e63811188badf0f7b9802c8963a}{free\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+pages()} also could be used to free compound frozen pages directly.

Return:\+ zero on success or negative error code.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{alloc\_contig\_pages\_noprof()}{alloc\_contig\_pages\_noprof()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{page__alloc_8c_ab9554147a97c722fc339570de7be13a3} 
struct page \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+pages\+\_\+noprof (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{unsigned long}]{nr\+\_\+pages}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp\+\_\+mask}{, }\item[{int}]{nid}{, }\item[{nodemask\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{nodemask}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+pages() -- tries to find and allocate contiguous range of pages @nr\+\_\+pages:\+ Number of contiguous pages to allocate @gfp\+\_\+mask:\+ GFP mask. @nid:\+ Target node @nodemask:\+ Mask for other possible nodes

This routine is a wrapper around alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+pages(), it can\textquotesingle{}t be used to allocate compound pages, the refcount of each allocated page will be set to one.

Allocated pages can be freed with \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_a5727806ce58adedcdb53501764ece724}{free\+\_\+contig\+\_\+range()} or by manually calling \+\_\+\+\_\+free\+\_\+page() on each allocated page.

Return:\+ pointer to contiguous pages on success, or NULL if not successful.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{alloc\_contig\_range\_noprof()}{alloc\_contig\_range\_noprof()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{page__alloc_8c_aec291e09dd27beda0c19d07892ebf743} 
int alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+range\+\_\+noprof (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{unsigned long}]{start}{, }\item[{unsigned long}]{end}{, }\item[{acr\+\_\+flags\+\_\+t}]{alloc\+\_\+flags}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp\+\_\+mask}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+range() -- tries to allocate given range of pages @start:\+ start PFN to allocate @end:\+ one-\/past-\/the-\/last PFN to allocate @alloc\+\_\+flags:\+ allocation information @gfp\+\_\+mask:\+ GFP mask.

This routine is a wrapper around alloc\+\_\+contig\+\_\+frozen\+\_\+range(), it can\textquotesingle{}t be used to allocate compound pages, the refcount of each allocated page will be set to one.

All pages which PFN is in \+[start, end) are allocated for the caller, and should be freed with \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_a5727806ce58adedcdb53501764ece724}{free\+\_\+contig\+\_\+range()} or by manually calling \+\_\+\+\_\+free\+\_\+page() on each allocated page.

Return:\+ zero on success or negative error code.

void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}\+\_\+\+\_\+meminit alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact\+\_\+nid\+\_\+noprof (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{nid}{, }\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{size}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp\+\_\+mask}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact\+\_\+nid -\/ allocate an exact number of physically-\/contiguous pages on a node. @nid:\+ the preferred node ID where memory should be allocated @size:\+ the number of bytes to allocate @gfp\+\_\+mask:\+ GFP flags for the allocation, must not contain \+\_\+\+\_\+\+GFP\+\_\+\+COMP

Like alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact(), but try to allocate on node nid first before falling back.

Return:\+ pointer to the allocated area or NULL in case of error.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{alloc\_pages\_exact\_noprof()}{alloc\_pages\_exact\_noprof()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{page__alloc_8c_a3d2f10fbb22a0a8e1cda8d12b74796b9} 
void \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact\+\_\+noprof (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{size\+\_\+t}]{size}{, }\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp\+\_\+mask}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact -\/ allocate an exact number physically-\/contiguous pages. @size:\+ the number of bytes to allocate @gfp\+\_\+mask:\+ GFP flags for the allocation, must not contain \+\_\+\+\_\+\+GFP\+\_\+\+COMP

This function is similar to alloc\+\_\+pages(), except that it allocates the minimum number of pages to satisfy the request. alloc\+\_\+pages() can only allocate memory in power-\/of-\/two pages.

This function is also limited by MAX\+\_\+\+PAGE\+\_\+\+ORDER.

Memory allocated by this function must be released by \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_aa0585d3acdf6b0d001ccaf5847a377a7}{free\+\_\+pages\+\_\+exact()}.

Return:\+ pointer to the allocated area or NULL in case of error.

\Hypertarget{page__alloc_8c_a8780a55d144abaf25c313e7d0aaf4333}\index{page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}!alloc\_pages\_nolock\_noprof@{alloc\_pages\_nolock\_noprof}}
\index{alloc\_pages\_nolock\_noprof@{alloc\_pages\_nolock\_noprof}!page\_alloc.c@{page\_alloc.c}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{alloc\_pages\_nolock\_noprof()}{alloc\_pages\_nolock\_noprof()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{page__alloc_8c_a8780a55d144abaf25c313e7d0aaf4333} 
struct page \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*} alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+nolock\+\_\+noprof (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{gfp\+\_\+t}]{gfp\+\_\+flags}{, }\item[{int}]{nid}{, }\item[{unsigned int}]{order}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+nolock -\/ opportunistic reentrant allocation from any context @gfp\+\_\+flags:\+ GFP flags. Only \+\_\+\+\_\+\+GFP\+\_\+\+ACCOUNT allowed. @nid:\+ node to allocate from @order:\+ allocation order size

Allocates pages of a given order from the given node. This is safe to call from any context (from atomic, NMI, and also reentrant allocator -\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} tracepoint -\/\texorpdfstring{$>$}{>} alloc\+\_\+pages\+\_\+nolock\+\_\+noprof). Allocation is best effort and to be expected to fail easily so nobody should rely on the success. Failures are not reported via \doxylink{page__alloc_8c_ac7f98d52df2f0026c70d73ef7ca2820b}{warn\+\_\+alloc()}. See always fail conditions below.

Return:\+ allocated page or NULL on failure. NULL does not mean EBUSY or EAGAIN. It means ENOMEM. There is no reason to call it again and expect !\+NULL.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{find\_next\_best\_node()}{find\_next\_best\_node()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{page__alloc_8c_a64edcc55ddb9eb0cfdcee87d1a7cc554} 
int find\+\_\+next\+\_\+best\+\_\+node (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{int}]{node}{, }\item[{nodemask\+\_\+t \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}}]{used\+\_\+node\+\_\+mask}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}

find\+\_\+next\+\_\+best\+\_\+node -\/ find the next node that should appear in a given node\textquotesingle{}s fallback list @node:\+ node whose fallback list we\textquotesingle{}re appending @used\+\_\+node\+\_\+mask:\+ nodemask\+\_\+t of already used nodes

We use a number of factors to determine which is the next node that should appear on a given node\textquotesingle{}s fallback list. The node should not have appeared already in @node\textquotesingle{}s fallback list, and it should be the next closest node according to the distance array (which contains arbitrary distance values from each node to each node in the system), and should also prefer nodes with no CPUs, since presumably they\textquotesingle{}ll have very little allocation pressure on them otherwise.

Return:\+ node id of the found node or NUMA\+\_\+\+NO\+\_\+\+NODE if no node is found.
